Shikyntien ha kaba pynkylla Kbasi Ta kane ka 
jingthoh diari jong u Sir J. Herbert Roberts M. P., 
uba la long Lord Clwyd pat hadiea jong ka ri Wales, 
u nongthoh u don bdn kiei-kiei kiba shon jingmut la 
u la ka ban leh kumta na ka bynta ka ri bad ki 
khdn ka ri ba lashai lashisngi.

Nyngkong eh, da kaba ai fa kane ha ka ri ha 
la ka kticn Khrsi, u ngeit ba kan larap fa kito kiba 
kwah ban buh Jingthoh fa ka jingtrei kam ka Mishon 
Wales ha ki ri Ldm Khasi jong ngi kum kawei ka 
bynta ne lynnong ha kata ka histori.

Ka baar ka long ban pyni ba ki khiin ka ri 
hadien habdd kum kaei ka jingsngew jong kiba wan 
peit na shabar fa ka ri jong ngi bad la ngi hi.

Ka balat ka long ba bdn na ki kyrteng briew 
ba la jia fakynduh bad u ha kane ka jingthoh diari jong u 
kin ai mynsiem fa ki pateng ki babdd ha khdn ha ksiew 
pateng la pateng,

Ka basaw ka long ban ai jingmut fa ki khdn riewstad 
ka ri hadien habud kumno ban nang thoh diari ne ban ai 
jingfathuh kiba pynsngewtynnat bad pyoioh jingmyntoi la 
ki nongpule pat sbadien shabdd.

Ka basan ka long ban kyrsiew ban nang pynriewspah 
fa ka thoh ka tar ha ki ri Ldm jong ngi, kaba ym 
shym la don wat tang la ki jong ki dak shuwa ka jingwan 
ai ki misfaoneri.

Shikyntien pat shaphang kine ki .nongiaid khmih 
pyrthei ba la khot ne la tip kum ki “Lai Sahep”, kiba la 
ialong M. P. pat hadien : —

Ka jingiaid khmih pyrthei hi ka kynthup 42 tylli ki 
lynnong bad na kita ki lynrong kiba dci ia ka ri Khasi ki 
long 5 tylli ki lynnong XlX-XXIIl.

U Sahep H. R. Davies, ha ka jingleit lynti lang jong 
ki, u khlad noh la ka lad na ki ar ngut ki Sahep tang shu 
mih na Japan ha ka lynnong XI IT, ha ka Sngi Balana 29 
tarik u Risaw 1884. bad u ia kynduh biang ia ki ha Sohra 
ha ka lynnong XXIII, ha ka lah Sngi u Blei 5 tarik u 
Kyllalyngkot 1885, kata, hadien kumba ar bnai tarn.

Kham bdnsien ia u Sahep J. Herbert Lewis la shu 
thoh lyngkot L — ha ka kot diari. Une u Sahep Lewis 
uha la ialong P., lang hadien, u la wan khmih biang ia 
ka Ri Khasi thik 40 snem hadien, ryngkat bad la ka mem 
bad u khdn samla jong u, uba la shon dur sincma kylleng 
la kylleng ka ri Khasi ban leit pyni pat sha ri Wales.
